Vegas, Nevada]]\n|attendance=1,909{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26522/tuf-14-finale-officially-draws-1909-attendance-and-453k-gate.mma|title=TUF 14 Finale officially draws 1,909 attendance and $453K gate|work=Mmajunkie.com|date=December 12, 2012|df=mdy-all}}\n|gate=$452,700\n|previousevent = [[UFC 139|UFC 139: Shogun vs. Henderson]]\n|followingevent = [[UFC 140|UFC 140: Jones vs. Machida]]\n}}\n\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter: Team Bisping vs. Team Miller Finale''''' (also known as '''''The Ultimate Fighter 14 Finale''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on December 3, 2011, at the [[Palms Casino Resort]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]].{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/event/TUF14-Finale#/fight|title=The Ultimate Fighter 14 Finale: Team Miller vs. Team Bisping|publisher=Ufc.com |date=October 3, 2011}}\n\n===Background===\nThe event featured the finalists from ''[[The Ultimate Fighter 14]]'' – Team Bisping vs Team Mayhem in the [[Featherweight (MMA)|Featherweight]] and [[Bantamweight (MMA)|Bantamweight]] divisions.\n\nWith [[Fox Sports (United States)|Fox Sports]] taking over UFC broadcasting rights in January 2012, this was the last UFC event to broadcast its main card on Spike TV. However, the network did broadcast two live preliminary fights at [[UFC 141]] later that month. The card was shown in the UK on FX.{{cite web|url=http://www.sportsbusinessdaily.com/Daily/Issues/2011/08/16/Media/UFC.aspx | title=UFC Agrees To Deal With Fox That Will See Up To Four Events Per Year On Broadcast TV | publisher=Sportsbusinessdaily.com | date=August 16, 2011}}\n\nThe preliminary bouts were streamed on Facebook and consisted entirely of cast members of the show.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26291/the-ultimate-fighter-14-finale-prelims-to-stream-on-facebook-heavy-on-cast-members.mma|title=The Ultimate Fighter 14 Finale prelims to stream on Facebook, heavy on cast members|publisher=Mmajunkie.com|date=November 29, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120101125012/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26291/the-ultimate-fighter-14-finale-prelims-to-stream-on-facebook-heavy-on-cast-members.mma|archive-date=January 1, 2012|df=mdy-all}}\n\n[[Akira Corassani]] was originally slated to fight [[Steven Siler]] at this event. However, Corassani suffered an injury and was forced to pull out of the bout.{{cite web |url=http://www.akirashow.com/2011/11/25/tuf-finale/ |title=AkiraShow > the TUF 14 Finale |access-date=2012-04-26 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120426011915/http://www.akirashow.com/2011/11/25/tuf-finale/ |archive-date=April 26, 2012 |df=mdy-all }} He was replaced by Josh Clopton.\n\nThis event also featured the debut of future Bantamweight Champion [[T.J. Night: Shields vs. Ellenberger]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 134: Silva vs. Okami''''' (also known as '''''UFC Rio''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] (MMA) [[pay-per-view]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on August 27, 2011, at the [[HSBC Arena (Rio de Janeiro)|HSBC Arena]] in [[Rio de Janeiro, Brazil]].{{cite web |url=https://www.ufc.com/event/UFC134#/fight|title=UFC 134: Silva vs. Okami 2|date=14 September 2018|publisher=ufc.com}}{{cite web |url=https://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-134-announcement-brazil|title=Silva vs. Okami II Headlines UFC 134 in Brazil|work=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC.com]] |date=June 16, 2011 |access-date=July 2, 2011}} The event was the second that the UFC has hosted in [[Brazil]], since 1998's [[UFC 17.5|UFC 17.5: Ultimate Brazil]]. UFC 134 was awarded the 2011 event of the year by [[Sherdog]].\n\n==Background==\n[[UFC 1]], [[UFC 2|2]], and [[UFC 4|4]] Tournament winner [[Royce Gracie]] was briefly linked to the event, but the rumors were refuted by UFC president [[Dana White]].{{cite web |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2011/04/19/dana-white-shoots-down-royce-gracie-ufc-134-return-rumors/|title=Dana White Shoots Down Royce Gracie UFC 134 Return Rumors|work=MMAFighting.com|date=April 19, 2011 |last=Chiappetta |first=Mike |access-date=July 2, 2011}}\n\n[[Maiquel Falcão]] was scheduled {{cite web |url=http://www.mmaentry.com/upcoming-mma-ufc/fight-card-event-details-results/ufc-134-silva-vs-okami-rio.html |title=UFC 134: Fight Card Lineup |work=MMAentry.com |date=August 13, 2011 |last=Samuels |first=Albert |access-date=August 17, 2011 }}{{Dead link|date=January 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot }} to take on [[Tom Lawlor]] at this event, but was released from the promotion in regard to a 2002 assault charge.{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/maiquel-falcao-released-from-the-ufc-bout-with-tom-lawlor-off-for-ufc-134|title=Maiquel Falcao released from the UFC, bout with Tom Lawlor off for UFC 134|publisher=MMAWeekly.com|date=May 11, 2011}} Lawlor was instead moved to [[UFC 139]] to take on [[Chris Weidman]].{{cite 4, 2011}}\n\n[[Mackens Semerzier]] was expected to face [[Iuri Alcântara]] at the event,{{cite Carvalho]].{{cite web |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Update-Pato-Carvalho-Joins-UFC-Faces-Alcantara-in-Rio-33225|title=Update: 'Pato' Carvalho Joins UFC, Faces Alcantara in Rio | by newcomer [[Felipe Arantes]].{{cite web |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Pato-Out-Chute-Boxe-Prospect-Sertanejo-in-at-UFC-134-33945|title='Pato' Out, Chute Boxe Prospect 'Sertanejo' in at UFC this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/23498/alexandre-cacareco-ferreire-vs-rousimar-palhares-in-the-works-for-ufc-134.mma|title=Alexandre \"Cacareco\" Ferreire vs. Rousimar Palhares in Brown Convention Center]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2011/04/29/next-ufc-fan-expo-set-for-oct-7-8-in-houston/ |title=Next UFC Fan Expo Set for Oct. 7–8 in Houston |work=MMAfighting.com |date=April 29, 2011 |last=Erickson |first=Matt |access-date=June 26, 2011}}{{Cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2011/5/25/2189681/ufc-fan-expo-tickets-for-houston-announced-for-george-r-brown|title=UFC Fan Expo tickets for Houston announced for George R. }}\n\n==Background==\n''UFC 136'' featured two preliminary fights live on [[Spike TV]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/24455/spike-tv-adds-ufc-prelims-special-for-ufc-136-edgar-vs-maynard-iii.mma |title=Spike TV adds \"UFC Prelims\" special for \"UFC 136: Edgar vs. Maynard III\" Grispi]] was expected to face [[Matt Grice]] at the event,{{cite web |url=http://www.mmaentry.com/upcoming-mma-ufc/fight-card-event-details-results/ufc-136-edgar-vs-maynard-3.html |title=UFC 136: Fight Card Lineup |work=MMAentry.com |date=August 13, 2011 |last=Samuels |first=Albert |access-date=August 17, 2011 }}{{Dead link|date=July 2025 |bot=InternetArchiveBot |fix-attempted=yes }} but Grispi was forced off the card with an injury and replaced by [[Nam Phan]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2011/10/3/2465647/ufc-136-fight-card-nam-phan-vs-leonard-garcia-preview|title=UFC 136 fight card: Nam Phan vs Leonard Garcia preview|date=October 2011}} Grice himself was also forced from the card with an undisclosed injury and replaced by [[Leonard Garcia]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25129/nam-phan-vs-leonard-garcia-ii-slated-for-ufc-136-in-houston.mma|date=September 7, 2011|title=Nam Phan vs. Leonard Garcia II slated for UFC 136 in Houston|work=MMAjunkie.com|access-date=September 7, 2012}}\n\n[[Aaron Simpson (fighter)|Aaron Simpson]] was expected to face [[Nick Catone]] at the event but Catone was forced off the card with an injury and replaced by [[Eric Schafer]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25174/eric-schafer-returns-to-ufc-meets-aaron-simpson-at-ufc-136.mma|date=September 10, 2011|title=Eric Schafer returns to UFC, meets Aaron Simpson at UFC 2013}}\n\n[[Mike Russow]] was scheduled to fight [[Dave Herman (fighter)|Dave Herman]] at the event, but the bout was cancelled after Herman failed his preliminary drug test in which he tested positive for [[marijuana]]. Herman denies using the prohibited substance.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25449/dave-herman-says-ufc-136-scratch-due-to-failed-drug-test-for-marijuana-steroids.mma|date=September 29, 2011|title=Dave Herman says UFC 136 scratch due to failed drug test for marijuana|work=mmajunkie.com|access-date=September 29, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://archive.today/20120712104343/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25449/dave-herman-says-ufc-136-scratch-due-to-failed-drug-test-for-marijuana-steroids.mma|archive-date=July 12, 2012}}\n\nThis event held the UFC record for most time spent in actual fighting at 158 minutes and 32 seconds, beating the last record holder ''[[UFC Fight Night: Maynard vs. Diaz]]'', which was 149 minutes and 50 seconds.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmatorch.com/artman2/publish/quicknews/article_10807.shtml|date=October 8, 2011|title=UFC 136 clocks in as longest event in UFC history|work=mmatorch.com}} ''[[UFC Fight Night: Werdum vs. Tybura]]'' would later break this record on November 19, 2017, with a total of 184 minutes and 18 seconds of cage time.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/11/ufc-fight-night-121-post-event-facts-a-long-night-leads-to-a-dubious-new-record|title=UFC Fight Night 121 post-event facts: A long night leads to a dubious new [[UFC 136|UFC 136: Edgar vs. Maynard III]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 138|UFC 138: Leben vs. Munoz]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 137: Penn vs. Diaz''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] (MMA) [[pay-per-view]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on October 29, 2011, at [[Mandalay Bay Events Center]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]].{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/event/UFC137#/fight|title=UFC 137: Penn vs. Diaz|publisher=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC.com]]|access-date=2011-10-18}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was originally expected to take place on October 15, 2011, at the [[Echo Arena Liverpool]] in [[Liverpool|Liverpool, England]].{{cite web| url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2011/04/27/ufc-returning-to-england-in-october/| title=UFC Returning to England in October| work=mmafighting.com| date=April 27, 2011 |last=Helwani |first=Ariel |author-link=Ariel Helwani |access-date=June 8, 2011}} However, due to multiple scheduling issues with broadcast rights, the organization cancelled those plans.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmamania.com/2011/6/8/2213179/ufc-has-a-less-than-20-percent-chance-of-bringing-an-event-to-the-uk|title=Marshall Zelaznik: UFC has a 'less than 20-percent chance' of bringing an event to the UK in 2011 |work=mmamania.com |date=June 8, 2011 |last=Holland |first=Jesse |access-date=June 8, 2011}} ''UFC 137'' featured two preliminary fights live on [[Spike TV]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/24912/ufc-137-gets-ufc-prelims-special-on-spike-tv.mma |title=UFC 137 gets \"UFC Prelims\" special on Spike TV |work=MMAjunkie.com |date=August 22, 2011 |last=Stupp |first=Dann |access-date=August 22, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20130128221249/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2011/08/ufc-137-gets-ufc-prelims-special-on-spike-tv |archive-date=January 28, 2013 }}\n\n[[Dennis Siver]] was expected to face [[Sam Stout]] at this event, but Stout withdrew from the bout on August 29 and was replaced by [[Donald Cerrone]].{{cite web |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/6907493/lightweight-donald-cerrone-replaces-sam-stout-ufc-137 |title=Donald Cerrone replaces Sam Stout |work=[[ESPN.com]] |date=August 29, 2011 |last=Okamoto |first=Brett |access-date=August 29, 2011}}\n\n[[Nick Diaz]], was originally scheduled to headline the card opposite [[Georges St-Pierre]] for the [[UFC Welterweight Championship]], but promoters canceled his appearance in the fight on September 7, because he failed to make media appearances. [[Carlos Condit]] was promoted from his fight with [[B.J. Penn]] to replace Diaz and face St-Pierre.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25127/nick-diaz-out-carlos-condit-in-against-champ-georges-st-pierre-at-ufc-137.mma |title=Nick Diaz out, Carlos Condit in against Champ Georges St-Pierre at UFC 137 |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=September 7, 2011 |access-date=September 7, 2011}}\n\nOn September 8, [[B.J. Penn]] announced via [[Twitter]] that [[Dana White]] offered him a fight he \"cannot refuse\" and will still be fighting at UFC 137. Later that day, both Penn and White announced that the fight would be against [[Nick Diaz]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25152/shocker-nick-diaz-rebooked-to-meet-b-j-penn-in-ufc-137-co-main-event.mma |title=Shocker: Nick Diaz rebooked to meet B.J. Penn in UFC 137 co-main event |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=September 8, 2011 |access-date=October 1, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110914113505/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25152/shocker-nick-diaz-rebooked-to-meet-b-j-penn-in-ufc-137-co-main-event.mma |archive-date=September 14, 2011 }}\n\nOn October 1, it was revealed that [[Tim Credeur]] was forced out of his bout with [[Brad Tavares]] for unknown reasons. Promotional newcomer [[Dustin Jacoby]] stepped-in to face Tavares.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25484/tim-credeur-out-dustin-jacoby-in-against-brad-tavares-at-ufc-137.mma |title=Tim Credeur out, Dustin Jacoby in against Brad Tavares at UFC 137 |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=October 1, 2011 |access-date=October 1, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20120711171540/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25484/tim-credeur-out-dustin-jacoby-in-against-brad-tavares-at-ufc-137.mma |archive-date=July 11, 2012 }} On October 21, Tavares withdrew from bout due to an injury and was replaced by undefeated newcomer [[Clifford Starks]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-137-fight-card-updates |title=Tavares out of 137; Starks to face Jacoby |publisher=UFC.com |date=October 21, 2011 |access-date=October 21, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20111012002630/http://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-137-fight-card-updates |archive-date=October 12, 2011 }}\n\n[[Georges St-Pierre]] was expected to defend his title against [[Carlos Condit]] on this card. St-Pierre withdrew due to a knee injury and [[Nick Diaz]] vs. [[B.J. Penn]] became the main event.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25715/ufc-champ-georges-st-pierre-out-of-ufc-137-headliner-with-injury.mma |title=UFC champ St-Pierre out of UFC 137 with injury, Diaz vs. Penn is named new headliner |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=October 18, 2011 |access-date=October 18, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20120711064255/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25715/ufc-champ-georges-st-pierre-out-of-ufc-137-headliner-with-injury.mma |archive-date=July 11, 2012 }}\n\nDuring the official UFC 137 weigh ins, [[Tyson Griffin]] failed to make the [[Featherweight (MMA)|featherweight]] limit. Griffin was three pounds over and was fined 25 percent of his earnings causing the bout to take place at a catchweight of 148 lb.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25840/ufc-137-penn-vs-diaz-live-and-official-weigh-in-results.mma |title=UFC 137 weigh-in results: Penn and Diaz make weight before brief scuffle |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=October 28, 2011 |access-date=October 28, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20111029120439/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25840/ufc-137-penn-vs-diaz-live-and-official-weigh-in-results.mma |archive-date=October 29, 2011 }}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Nick Diaz]]\n|def.\n|[[B.J. '''Mirko Cro Cop:''' $75,000\n*'''Scott Jorgensen:''' $33,000 ($16,500 win bonus) def. '''Jeff Curran:''' $8,000\n*'''Hatsu Hioki:''' $30,000 ($15,000 win bonus) def. '''George Roop:''' $8,000\n*'''Donald Cerrone:''' $54,000 ($27,000 win bonus) def. '''Dennis Siver:''' $27,000\n*'''Bart Palaszewski:''' $28,500 ($10,000 win bonus) def. '''Tyson Griffin:''' $25,500 ^\n*'''Brandon Vera:''' $120,000 ($60,000 win bonus) def. '''Eliot Marshall:''' $15,000\n*'''Ramsey Nijem:''' $20,000 ($10,000 win bonus) def. '''Danny Downes:''' $5,000\n*'''Francis Carmont:''' $12,000 ($6,000 win bonus) def. '''Chris Camozzi:''' $8,000\n*'''Clifford Starks:''' $12,000 ($6,000 win bonus) def. '''Dustin Jacoby:''' $6,000\n\n^''Although not recognized on the official pay sheet, Griffin was fined $8,500, or 25% of his base pay for missing weight for the fight, and contracts were reworked with those amounts. Zuffa, the UFC's parent company, was not the owner back then. UFC president Dana White, in an interview with MMAWeekly.com, says that the company has long been on a course to return to Denver, but they wanted to do it the right way. “We knew that we wanted to bring a big exciting card back to Denver, and we finally got it done.”{{cite web |url= http://mmaweekly.com/ufc-president-dana-white-we-always-planned-to-come-back-to-denver |title=UFC President Dana White: We Always Planned to Come Back to Denver |date=July 24, 2011 |publisher=MMAWeekly.com |access-date=2011-07-24}}\n\n[[Jon Jones]] was expected to make his first title defense on August 6, 2011, at [[UFC 133]] against [[Rashad Evans]], but Jones was sidelined with a hand injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/jon-bones-jones-injured-rashad-evans-faces-phil-davis-at-ufc-133|title=Jon \"Bones\" Jones Injured|publisher=MMAWeekly.com|date=April 25, 2011}} It was initially announced that the hand injury would require surgery, but Jones opted for rest and rehabilitation without surgery after further consultations with doctors.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2011/05/13/jon-jones-elects-to-not-have-surgery-on-injured-hand/ |title=Jon Jones Elects to Not Have Surgery on Injured Hand |last=Helwani |first=Ariel |publisher=MMAFighting.com |date=2011-05-13 |access-date=2011-05-16}} Jones' injury was originally thought to keep him out of action until late 2011, but instead, he made his first title defense against [[Rampage Jackson|Quinton Jackson]] at this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/24004/as-expected-champ-jones-and-challenger-rampage-headline-ufc-135.mma|title=As expected, champ Jon Jones and challenger \"Rampage\" Jackson headline UFC 135|publisher=mmajunkie|date=June 15, 2011}}\n\n''UFC 135'' featured two preliminary fights live on [[Spike TV]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/24453/spike-tv-adds-ufc-prelims-special-for-ufc-135-jones-vs-rampage.mma |title=Spike TV adds \"UFC Prelims\" special for \"UFC 135: Jones vs. Rampage\" |publisher=[[MMAjunkie.com]] |date=July 18, 2011 |last=Morgan |first=John |access-date=July 18, 2011 |url-status=dead url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Nunes-Confirms-UFC-135-Exit-Awaits-Gamburyan-in-December-35155 | title=Nunes confirms UFC 135 exit, awaits Gamburyan in December. | publisher=Sherdog.com | Page]] was expected for this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/23982/bantamweights-kid-yamamoto-vs-damacio-page-targeted-for-ufc-135.mma|title=Bantamweights \"Kid\" Yamamoto vs. Damacio martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on November 5, 2011, at [[LG Arena (Birmingham)|LG Arena]] in [[Birmingham]], United Kingdom.{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/event/UFC138#/fight|title=UFC 138: Leben vs. Muñoz|publisher=ufc.com |date=September 6, 2011 |access-date=September 6, 2011}} The event aired on the same day, via [[Broadcast delay|tape delay]], on [[Spike (TV channel)|Spike TV]] in the United States and live on [[Sportsnet]] in Canada.{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-138-fight-card-updates|title=UFC 138 Fight Card Updates| work=ufc.com |date=July 19, 2011 |access-date=July 19, 2011}} The event was the second that the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] has hosted in [[Birmingham]], the first was [[UFC 89]] back in 2008 also featuring [[Chris Leben]] in the main event.\n\n==Background==\nAfter several tentatively scheduled 2011 events {{cite web |url=http://www.mmaentry.com/upcoming-mma-ufc/fight-card-event-details-results/ufc-138-leben-vs-munoz.html |title=UFC 138: Fight Card Lineup |work=MMAentry.com |date=August 13, 2011 |last=Samuels |first=Albert |access-date=August 17, 2011 }}{{Dead link|date=July 2025 |bot=InternetArchiveBot |fix-attempted=yes }} at various locations around the United Kingdom did not materialize, including [[Glasgow]], [[Liverpool]], London as well as a planned return to Ireland, it was expected that the organization would not hold an event in the area during 2011.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2011/6/8/2213179/ufc-has-a-less-than-20-percent-chance-of-bringing-an-event-to-the-uk|title=Marshall Zelaznik: UFC has a 'less than 20-percent chance' of bringing an event to the UK in 2011 |work=mmamania.com|date=June 8, 2011 |last=Holland |first=Jesse |access-date=June 8, 2011}}\n\nThe main event was the first non-title five round fight in the history of the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2011/07/19/leben-vs-munoz-will-be-five-round-fight-at-ufc-138-in-england/|title=Leben vs. Muñoz Will Be Five-Round Fight| work=mmafighting.com |date=July 19, 2011 |last=Helwani |first=Ariel |access-date=July 19, 2011}}\n\nMark Scanlon was expected to face [[James Head (fighter)|James Head]] at this event.{{cite web | url=http://www.yourmma.tv/news/details.asp/id/1648/scanlon-vs-head-ufc-138.htm | title=Scanlon vs. Head To Feature At UFC 138 | publisher=YourMMA.tv | date=July 21, 2011 | access-date=July 21, 2011 | url-status=usurped | archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120313034104/http://www.yourmma.tv/news/details.asp/id/1648/scanlon-vs-head-ufc-138.htm | archive-date=March 13, 2012 }} However, Scanlon was pulled from the bout and replaced by promotional newcomer [[John Maguire (fighter)|John Maguire]]{{cite web|url= http://mmajunkie.com/news/25330/mark-scanlon-out-of-ufc-138-john-maguire-debuts-against-james-head.mma|title= Mark Scanlon out of UFC 138, John Maguire debuts against James Head|access-date= 2010-09-21|work= mmajunkie.com|url-status= dead|archive-url= himself pulled out of the fight due to an undisclosed injury and was replaced by [[Justin Edwards (fighter)|Justin Edwards]].{{cite web|url=http://www.thefightlounge.co.uk/head-out-edwards-in-ufc-138/|title=James Head out, Justin Edwards in|publisher=The Fight Lounge|date=September 28, expected to face [[John Hathaway (fighter)|John Hathaway]] at this event.{{cite web|url= http://mmajunkie.com/news/24530/john-hathaway-vs-pascal-krauss-set-for-ufc-138-in-england.mma|title= John the bout citing a shoulder injury, and was replaced by [[Matt Brown (fighter)|Matt Brown]].{{cite web|url= that Hathaway was forced to withdraw from the bout due to injury as well. As a result, Brown was pulled from the event and will face [[Seth Baczynski]] at [[UFC 139]].{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-138-fight-card-updates?139|title= Hathaway, Thompson out of UFC 138|access-date= 2010-10-17 |work= UFC.com}}{{cite web|url= http://mmajunkie.com/news/25705/after-injuries-de-fries-broughton-slated-for-ufc-138-brown-baczynski-at-ufc-139.mma|title= After injuries, Brown-Baczynski at UFC 139|access-date= 2010-10-17|work= mmajunkie.com|url-status= dead|archive-url= https://archive.today/20120712141548/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25705/after-injuries-de-fries-broughton-slated-for-ufc-138-brown-baczynski-at-ufc-139.mma|archive-date= 2012-07-12}}\n\n[[Phil De Fries]] was expected to face [[Oli Thompson]] at this event. However, on October 17 it was announced that Thompson was forced to withdraw from the bout due to injury. De Fries fought [[Rob Broughton]] at this event instead.\n\n[[Anthony Njokuani]] was scheduled to fight [[Paul Taylor (fighter)|Paul Taylor]] on the main card. However, Taylor sustained a [[whiplash (medicine)|whiplash]] injury during a minor traffic collision on November 1 forcing him from the bout. '''''UFC on Versus 6''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on October 1, 2011, at the [[Capital One Arena|Verizon Center]] in [[Washington, D.C.]]{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/event/UVS6#/fight|title=UFC Live: Cruz vs. Johnson|publisher=ufc.com}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event featured a [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between champion [[Dominick Cruz]] and #1 contender [[Demetrious Johnson]], which was the first UFC title fight to be aired live at a free televised UFC event since [[UFC 75]] in September 2007.\n\n[[Fabio Maldonado]] was expected to face [[Aaron Rosa]] in a Light Heavyweight bout, but was pulled from the card due to an injury. A replacement was being sought but this fight was scrapped.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25236/fabio-maldonado-scratched-from-ufc-on-versus-6-due-to-injury.mma|title=Fabio Maldonado scratched from UFC on Versus 6 due to injury|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=September 14, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110928075935/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25236/fabio-maldonado-scratched-from-ufc-on-versus-6-due-to-injury.mma|archive-date=September 28, 2011}}\n\n[[Jeff Hougland]] was expected to face [[Mike Easton]] in a Bantamweight bout, but has been injured and replaced by UFC newcomer Byron Bloodworth.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25431/jeff-hougland-out-byron-bloodworth-in-against-mike-easton-at-ufc-on-versus-6.mma|title=Jeff Hougland out, Byron Bloodworth in against Mike Easton at UFC on Versus 6|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=September 27, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110930114207/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25431/jeff-hougland-out-byron-bloodworth-in-against-mike-easton-at-ufc-on-versus-6.mma|archive-date=September 30, 2011}} Bloodworth failed to make the 135 pound weight limit, thus the match was changed to a Catchweight bout, with Bloodworth being fined for the infraction.{{cite web |url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Cruz-Johnson-Cleared-for-UFC-Live-6-Title-Showdown-36066|title=Cruz, Johnson Cleared for UFC LIve 6 Title Showdown|publisher=sherdog.com|date= September 30, 2011}}\n\nWith [[Fox Broadcasting Company|Fox]] taking over UFC broadcasting rights in 2012, this was the last UFC and [[Zuffa]] event to be aired on [[Versus (TV channel)|Versus]].\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Versus)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Dominick Cruz]] (c)\n|def\n|[[Demetrious Johnson]]\n|Decision (unanimous) [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on November 19, 2011, at the [[HP Pavilion at San Jose|HP Pavilion]] in [[San Jose, California|San Jose]], [[California]].{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/event/UFC139#/fight|title=UFC 139: Shogun vs. Henderson|publisher=ufc.com |date=September 28, 2011 |access-date=September 28, 2011}} It was the first [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] event in [[San Jose, California|San Jose]], the longtime home and base arena for [[Strikeforce (mixed martial arts)|Strikeforce]].\n\n==Background==\n''UFC 139'' featured two preliminary fights live on [[Spike TV]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/24921/ufc-139-gets-ufc-prelims-special-on-spike-tv.mma |title=UFC 139 gets \"UFC Prelims\" special on Spike TV |work=[[MMAjunkie.com]] |date=August 23, 2011 |last=Stupp |first=Dann |access-date=August 23, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20130129072026/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2011/08/ufc-139-gets-ufc-prelims-special-on-spike-tv |archive-date=January 29, 2013 }}\n\n[[List of UFC champions#Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Cain Velasquez]] was originally expected to have his first title defense against [[Junior dos Santos]] at this event.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2011/08/04/cain-velasquez-set-to-defend-ufc-title-vs-junior-dos-santos-at/|title=Cain Velasquez Set to Defend UFC Title vs. Junior Dos Santos at UFC 139| work=mmafighting.com |date=August 4, 2011 |access-date=August 4, 2011}} However, on September 2, 2011, the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] announced that fight would be moved to the inaugural [[UFC on Fox 1|UFC on Fox]] event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25064/xx-vs-xx-headlines-ufcs-debut-on-fox.mma|title=Cain Velasquez vs. Junior Dos Santos headlines UFC's debut on FOX|work=mmajunkie.com|date=September 2, 2011|access-date=September 2, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120317042333/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25064/xx-vs-xx-headlines-ufcs-debut-on-fox.mma|archive-date=March 17, 2012}}\n\nTo fill in the vacancy left by Cain vs dos Santos, [[Dana White]] confirmed on September 20, 2011 that [[Dan Henderson]] had re-signed with the UFC and would fight [[Mauricio Rua|Maurício \"Shogun\" Rua]] for the main event.{{cite news |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2011/09/21/dana-white-confirms-dan-henderson-vs-shogun-rua-at-ufc-139|title=Dana White Confirms Dan Henderson vs. Shogun Rua at UFC 139\n|publisher=mmafighting.com |date=September 21, 2011 |access-date=August 21, 2018}} White also declared that Shogun vs. Henderson would be a \"No. 1 contender’s match\" for the light heavyweight title. However, White clarified that [[Rashad Evans]] was still planned to get the title shot against the winner of [[UFC 135|Jon Jones vs Rampage Jackson]], and that the winner of Shogun vs. Henderson would then get the second crack at the title.{{cite news |url=https://www.mmaweekly.com/dan-henderson-vs-shogun-rua-winner-gets-next-shot-at-the-title-after-rashad-evans|title=Dan Henderson vs. Shogun Rua Winner Gets Next Shot at the Title after Rashad Evans| publisher=mmaweekly.com |date=September 21, 2011 |access-date=August 21, 2018}}\n\n[[Vitor Belfort]] was briefly linked to a matchup with [[Cung Le]] at this event.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25173/vitor-belfort-says-hes-fighting-cung-le-at-ufc-139-in-san-jose.mma |title=Vitor Belfort says he's fighting Cung Le at UFC 139 in San Jose |access-date=2011-09-10 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20120710154524/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25173/vitor-belfort-says-hes-fighting-cung-le-at-ufc-139-in-san-jose.mma |archive-date=2012-07-10 }} However, Belfort was removed from the bout and replaced by [[Wanderlei Silva]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25329/ufc-139-shakeup-vitor-belfort-out-wanderlei-silva-in-against-cung-le.mma |title=UFC 139 shakeup: Vitor Belfort out, Wanderlei Silva in against Cung Le |access-date=2011-09-21 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20130104013241/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2011/09/ufc-139-shakeup-vitor-belfort-out-wanderlei-silva-in-against-cung-le |archive-date=2013-01-04 }}\n\n[[Johnny Eduardo]] was expected to face [[Michael McDonald (mixed martial artist)|Michael McDonald]] at the event.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25433/ufc-139-adds-mcdonald-eduardo-bout-torres-pace-confirmed-for-san-jose-event.mma |title=UFC 139 adds McDonald-Eduardo bout, Torres-Pace confirmed for San Jose event |access-date=2011-09-28 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |archive-date=2011-10-01 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20111001023613/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25433/ufc-139-adds-mcdonald-eduardo-bout-torres-pace-confirmed-for-san-jose-event.mma |url-status=dead }} However, Eduardo was forced from the card with a shoulder injury on November 4{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25954/johnny-eduardo-scratched-from-ufc-139-replacement-search-underway.mma |title=Johnny Eduardo scratched from UFC 139, replacement search underway |access-date=2011-11-04 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20111106144403/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25954/johnny-eduardo-scratched-from-ufc-139-replacement-search-underway.mma |archive-date=2011-11-06 }} and replaced by promotional newcomer Alex Soto.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26012/newcomer-alex-soto-meets-michael-mcdonald-at-next-weeks-ufc-139-event.mma |title=Newcomer Alex Soto meets Michael McDonald at next week's UFC 139 event |access-date=2011-11-09 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20120726162102/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26012/newcomer-alex-soto-meets-michael-mcdonald-at-next-weeks-ufc-139-event.mma |archive-date=2012-07-26 }}\n\nIt was initially suspected that the first [[Spike (TV network)|Spike TV]] preliminary fight would feature [[Tom Lawlor]] vs. [[Chris Weidman]], but it became known that it was [[Michael McDonald (mixed martial artist)|Michael McDonald]] fighting [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] newcomer Alex Soto instead.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2011/11/14/after-reshuffling-ufc-139-spike-prelims-to-feature-bader-vs-br/?a_dgi=aolshare_twitter |title=After Reshuffling, UFC 139 Spike Prelims to Feature Bader vs. Brilz, McDonald vs. Soto |date=14 November 2011 |access-date=2011-11-14 |publisher=mmafighting.com}}\n\nDuring the official UFC 139 weigh ins, [[Shamar Bailey]] and [[Nick Pace]] failed to make the weight limit for their respective weight classes. Bailey came in 3 pounds over, while Pace came in six pounds over. Each fighter was fined 20 percent of their earnings and their bouts took at catchweights of 158 lb and 141 lb respectively.{{cite web | critics. The main event drew many accolades, including UFC President Dana White calling it \"one of the top three best fights ever in MMA\", later comparing the fight to the [[Thrilla in Manila]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/7259739/dan-henderson-hangs-decision-mauricio-shogun-rua-ufc-139|title = Henderson hangs on to decision Rua at UFC 139|date = 20 November 2011}}{{Cite web|url=http://www.espn.co.uk/ufc/sport/story/122297.html|title = MMA's own Thrilla in Manila was a draw - White}} The fight would later garner numerous Fight of the Year awards.{{cite web| url = https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/7431228/mma-awards-2011| title = ESPN.com MMA awards for 2011}}{{cite web| url=http://blog.chron.com/fighting/2012/01/2011-brawl-sports-awards-fight-of-the-year/ | title=2011 Brawl Sports Awards: Fight of the Year | archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160306183330/http://blog.chron.com/sportsupdate/2012/01/2011-brawl-sports-awards-fight-of-the-year/ | archive-date=2016-03-06}}{{Cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2011/12/24/fight-of-the-year-dan-henderson-vs-shogun-rua|title = Fight of the Year: Dan Henderson vs. 'Shogun' Rua|date = 24 December 2011}} The fight was later included in the [[UFC Hall of Fame]] in 2018.{{Cite web |last=Raimondi |first=Marc |date=2018-05-12 |title=Dan Henderson vs. 'Shogun' Rua I to be inducted into UFC Hall of Fame |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2018/5/12/17348512/dan-henderson-vs-shogun-rua-i-to-be-inducted-into-ufc-hall-of-fame |access-date=2022-11-19 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\n==Bonus awards==\nThe following fighters received $70,000 bonuses.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26198/ufc-139-bonuses-earn-awards.mma|title=UFC 139 bonuses: McDonald, Faber, Henderson, 'Shogun,' Silva, Le earn $70K awards|work=mmajunkie.com|date=2010-11-20|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://archive.today/20120718191411/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26198/ufc-139-bonuses-earn-awards.mma|archive-date=2012-07-18}}\n\n* '''Fight of the Night: Maurício Rua vs. Dan Henderson''' and '''Wanderlei Silva vs. Cung Le'''\n* '''Knockout of the Night: Michael McDonald'''\n* '''Submission of the Night: Urijah Faber'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[California State Athletic Commission]]. Matt Brown: $12,000\n*'''Danny Castillo:''' $34,000 ($17,000 win bonus) def. Shamar Bailey: $8,000 ^\n\n^ ''Shamar Bailey and Nick Pace were reportedly fined 20 percent of their purse for failing to make the required weight for their respective fights. The CSAC's initial report did not include information on the penalty.''\n\n==References==\n{{reflist|30em}}\n\n{{UFC Events}}\n{{UFC Hall of Fame}}\n\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship flagship events]]\n[[Category:2010s in San Jose, California]]\n[[Category:2011 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts events in San Jose, California]]\n[[Category:November 2011 sports events in the United States]]\n[[Category:2011 in sports in California]]"}}}],"langlinks":[{"lang":"ja","title":"UFC 139"}]},{"pageid":32622020,"ns":0,"title":"UFC 140","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2011}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC 140: Jones vs. Machida\n|image=UFC140 PRESALE JPG.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date=December 10, 2011\n|venue=[[Air Canada Centre]]\n|city= [[Toronto]], [[Ontario]], Canada\n|attendance=18,303\n|gate=$3,900,000\n|buyrate=\n|previousevent= [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Bisping vs. Team Miller Finale]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC 141|UFC 141: Lesnar vs. Overeem]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 140: Jones vs. Machida''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on December 10, 2011, at the [[Air Canada Centre]] in [[Toronto]], [[Ontario]], Canada. It was the penultimate event for the UFC in 2011. The event featured eight bouts televised internationally, seven preliminary bouts aired on cable in the United States and Canada, and three preliminary bouts streamed live on [[Facebook]].\n\nIn the co-main event, [[Frank Mir]], who was the first man to knock out [[Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira]] at [[UFC 92]], became the first man to submit Nogueira. Mir accomplished the feat with a [[kimura lock|kimura]] that ultimately broke Nogueira's arm. In the main event, [[Lyoto Machida]] also suffered his first submission loss at the hands of [[Jon Jones]]. In doing so, Jones retained the [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] title.\n\n==Background==\nA bout between [[Lyoto Machida]] and [[Phil Davis (fighter)|Phil Davis]] was initially announced by UFC President, [[Dana White]], to happen at ''UFC 140''. However, White, after discovering that Davis was still recovering from a knee injury, admitted to \"[jumping] the gun\" on the announcement .{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25430/ufc-140-adds-phil-davis-vs-lyoto-machida.mma |title=UFC 140 briefly adds Phil Davis vs. Lyoto Machida, but UFC boss 'jumped gun' |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=September 27, 2011 |access-date=March 25, 2012 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110929135828/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25430/ufc-140-adds-phil-davis-vs-lyoto-machida.mma |archive-date=September 29, 2011 }} Early reports also included a Light Heavyweight Championship bout between [[Jon Jones]] and [[Rashad Evans]]. First, Evans would need to be medically cleared following an operation that required pins to be inserted into his right hand due to an injury during his technical knockout victory over [[Tito Ortiz]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2011/10/05/ufc-targeting-jon-jones-vs-rashad-evans-for-ufc-140-main-event |title=UFC Targeting Jon Jones vs. Rashad Evans for UFC 140 Main Event |first=Mike |last=Chiappetta |publisher=MMAFighting.com |date=October 5, 2012 |access-date=March 25, 2012}} When it became clear that Evans would not be ready for the fight, he was replaced by Machida for the championship bout.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25561/ufc-140-headliner-features-champ-jon-jones-vs-lyoto-machida.mma |title=UFC 140 headliner features champ Jon Jones vs. Lyoto Machida |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=October 7, 2011 |access-date=March 25, 2012 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20120730203447/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25561/ufc-140-headliner-features-champ-jon-jones-vs-lyoto-machida.mma |archive-date=July 30, 2012 }}\n\nJon Jones had won the [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] earlier in the year after defeating [[Maurício Rua]] at ''[[UFC 128]]'' by technical knockout. Six months later, Jones defended the title after submitting [[Rampage Jackson|Quinton Jackson]], a former UFC Light Heavyweight Champion, at ''[[UFC 135]]''. Lyoto Machida was himself a former UFC Light Heavyweight champion, having won the title via a knock out win over [[Rashad Evans]] at ''[[UFC 98]]'' in 2009. Machida lost the title a year later to Maurício Rua at ''[[UFC 113]]'', the same man from whom Jones won the title in Rua's next outing. Machida's only career losses were to Rua and Jackson, both of whom Jones had beaten earlier in 2011.{{cite news |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/7067623/jon-jones-gets-lyoto-machida-ufc-light-heavy-defense |title=Jon Jones' defense vs. Lyoto Machida |first=Franklin |last=McNeil |publisher=ESPN |date=October 6, 2012 |access-date=March 25, 2012}}\n\nThe co-main event was a rematch between [[Frank Mir]] and [[Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira]]. The two had fought about three years prior, when Nogueira was the [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|Interim UFC Heavyweight Champion]], at ''[[UFC 92]]'' . In that match, Mir became the first person to defeat Nogueria by stoppage, due to a technical knockout in the second round.{{cite news |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/6941597/rematch-frank-mir-antonio-rodrigo-nogueira-set-ufc-140 |title=Frank Mir to fight at UFC 140 |first=Brett |last=Okamoto |publisher=ESPN |date=September 7, 2011 |access-date=March 26, 2012}}\n\nAnother headlining fight involved Nogueira's twin brother, [[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]], who faced off against [[Tito Ortiz]]. The pair were originally scheduled to fight at a [[UFC Fight Night: Nogueira vs. Davis|UFC Fight Night]] in March 2011. However, when Ortiz withdrew due to an injury, he was replaced by [[Phil Davis (fighter)|Phil Davis]]. Plans for ''UFC 140'' initially paired Ortiz against [[Rich Franklin]]. Shoulder surgery required Franklin to withdraw from the fight; Franklin was replaced by Ortiz's original opponent from eight months prior, Nogueira.{{cite web |url=https://www.usatoday.com/sports/mma/post/2011-09-09/ortiz-vs-lil-nog-added-to-ufc-140/546705/1 |title=Ortiz vs. Lil Nog added to UFC 140 |first=Sergio |last=Non |publisher=USA Today |date=September 9, 2011 |access-date=March 26, 2012}}\n\n[[Rory MacDonald (fighter)|Rory MacDonald]] was expected to face [[Brian Ebersole]] at the event. However, on November 8, MacDonald pulled out of the bout because of injury and was replaced by [[Claude Patrick]]. Patrick was originally scheduled to face [[Rich Attonito]] on the preliminary card of the event.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26004/rory-macdonald-out-claude-patrick-in-against-brian-ebersole-at-ufc-140.mma |title=Rory MacDonald out, Claude Patrick in against Brian Ebersole at UFC 140 |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=November 8, 2011 |access-date=November 8, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20111110013806/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26004/rory-macdonald-out-claude-patrick-in-against-brian-ebersole-at-ufc-140.mma |archive-date=November 10, 2011 }} [[Rich Attonito]] remained on the preliminary card and faced UFC newcomer [[Jake Hecht]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26041/rich-attonito-meets-newcomer-jake-hecht-at-ufc-140.mma |title=Rich Attonito meets newcomer Jake Hecht at UFC 140 |access-date=2011-11-10 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20111112055551/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26041/rich-attonito-meets-newcomer-jake-hecht-at-ufc-140.mma |archive-date=2011-11-12 }} [[Dennis Hallman]] missed the weight allowance for his lightweight return, weighing in at 158.5 pounds. He was fined 20% of his earnings for the fight and his fight with [[John Makdessi]] was subsequently changed to a [[catch weight]] bout.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26446/ufc-140-live-and-official-weigh-in-results.mma|title=UFC 140 weigh-in results|work=mmajunkie.com|date=December 9, 2011|access-date=December 9, 2011|archive-date=July 20, 2012|archive-url=https://archive.today/20120720132822/mmajunkie.com/news/26446/ufc-140-live-and-official-weigh-in-results.mma|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Event==\n''UFC 140'' consisted of twelve mixed martial arts bouts sanctioned by Ontario's Athletics Commissioner under the MMA rules established by the [[New Jersey State Athletic Control Board]] and often referred to as the [[Unified Rules of Mixed Martial Arts]].{{cite press release |url=http://news.ontario.ca/mcs/en/2010/08/mixed-martial-arts-in-ontario.html |title=Mixed Martial Arts In Ontario |publisher=Ontario, Canada |date=August 14, 2010 |access-date=March 27, 2012}} The non-championship bouts were held for three five-minute rounds. The championship bout between Jon Jones and Lyoto Machida was scheduled for five five-minute rounds. The event was planned to feature four preliminary bouts live on [[Ion Television]] and on [[Sportsnet]] in Canada{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-140-Prelim-Bouts-to-Air-on-Ion-Television |title=UFC 140 Prelim Bouts Air on ION Television |access-date=December 5, 2011 |publisher=UFC}} as well as three preliminary bouts streamed on [[Facebook]]. Due to the early end of the televised preliminary fights, the three fights on Facebook were also televised on cable. The attendance at [[Air Canada Centre]] was reported to be just over 18,000, bringing in almost $4 million from ticket sales.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26498/ufc-140-draws-a-reported-18303-attendees-for-3-9-million-live-gate.mma |title=UFC 140 draws a reported 18,303 attendees for $3.9 million live gate |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=December 11, 2011 |access-date=March 27, 2012 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20120711155807/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26498/ufc-140-draws-a-reported-18303-attendees-for-3-9-million-live-gate.mma |archive-date=July 11, 2012 }} The [[pay-per-view]] buyrate was reported to be 485,000.{{cite web |url=http://mmapayout.com/blue-book/pay-per-view/ |title=Pay-per-view : MMAPayout.com: The Business of MMA |publisher=MMAPayout.com |access-date=March 27, 2012 |archive-date=June 27, 2011 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110627142203/http://mmapayout.com/blue-book/pay-per-view/ |url-status=dead }}\n\n===Preliminary card===\nThe first fight of the evening was between [[Mitch Clarke]] and [[John Cholish]]. In the second round, Clarke attempted to take down Cholish. During the scramble, Cholish managed to take Clarke's back. Cholish flattened Clarke out and began striking Clarke until referee [[John McCarthy (mixed martial arts)|John McCarthy]] stopped the fight.{{cite news |url=https://www.usatoday.com/sports/mma/post/2011-12-10/ufc-140-prelims/582524/1 |title=UFC 140: Pokrajac, Philippou, Hecht win early fights |first=Sergio |last=Non |publisher=USA Today |date=December 10, 2011 |access-date=March 27, 2012}} Cholish won the fight at 4:36 of the second round, due to a technical knockout, giving Clarke his first ever professional MMA loss.\n\n[[Rich Attonito]] faced [[Jake Hecht]] in the second bout of the night. Attonito controlled the fight in the first round following a takedown and damaged Hecht while on top of him.{{cite web |url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2011/12/11/2628761/ufc-140-results-recap-rich-attonito-vs-jake-hecht |title=UFC 140 Results Recap: Rich Attonito vs. Jake Hecht |first=Brent |last=Brookhouse |publisher=BloodyElbow.com |date=December 11, 2011 |access-date=March 27, 2012 }}{{Dead link|date=January 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot }} Attonito attempted a take down again in the second round, but Hecht hit Attonito in the temple with elbow strikes while defending the takedown. Attonito went down and Hecht continued to punch him until the referee ended the fight. Jake Hecht, in his UFC debut, defeated Rich Attonito by technical knockout at 1:10 of the second round.\n\nCanadian native [[Mark Bocek]]'s fight with [[Nik Lentz]] lasted the full three rounds. Bocek was able to take Lentz down at will and was consistently on top of Lentz throughout the fight. Lentz was not very active from the bottom, although he was able to prevent Bocek from passing his guard. Lentz attempted to apply a guillotine choke several times, but was unsuccessful in submitting Bocek. The judges' decision went to Mark Bocek, 30–27, resulting in Lentz suffering his first loss in the UFC.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26488/ufc-140-results-mark-bocek-vs-nik-lentz.mma |title=UFC 140 results: Mark Bocek delivers Nik Lentz his first UFC loss |first1=Dann |last1=Stupp |first2=John |last2=Morgan |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=December 10, 2011 |access-date=March 27, 2012 }}{{Dead link|date=January 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot }}\n\nIn the first bout of the night that aired on cable television, [[Yves Jabouin]] faced [[Walel Watson]] in the second fight of the night to go the full distance. Most of the fight was held with both fighters on their feet. Watson made use of his longer reach and height to strike from a distance, with Jabouin attempting to get in close to land harder strikes. The second and third rounds also saw Watson attempt to submit his opponent with chokes.{{cite web |url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2011/12/11/2628926/ufc-140-results-recap-yves-jabouin-vs-walel-watson |title=UFC 140 Results Recap: Yves Jabouin vs. Walel Watson |first=Brent |last=Brookhouse |publisher=BloodyElbow.com |date=December 11, 2011 |access-date=March 27, 2012 |archive-date=March 4, 2016 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160304071706/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2011/12/11/2628926/ufc-140-results-recap-yves-jabouin-vs-walel-watson |url-status=dead }} In the end, the judges scored the bout a split decision in favor of Yves Jabouin.\n\n[[John Makdessi]] then faced [[Dennis Hallman]] in a catchweight bout due to Hallman's failure to make weight for the fight. Within seconds of the start of the fight, Hallman was able to take Makdessi down, mounted him and began to [[ground and pound]] Makdessi. Makdessi rolled over in an attempt to defend himself, resulting in Hallman taking his back and applying a rear-naked choke. Dennis Hallman gave John Makdessi his first career loss due to submission with the rear-naked choke at 2:58 of the first round. Afterwards, Hallman stated his win should have an asterisk, since he was overweight for the fight.\n\nA middleweight fight between [[Jared Hamman]] and [[Costas Philippou]] also ended in the first round. Philippou was able repeatedly to hit Hamman with powerful right hand punches causing Hamman to fall to the mat at least four times. The last punch resulted in Hamman falling face first to the mat, at which point John McCarthy ended the fight. Costas Philippou earned his second straight UFC victory by defeating Jared Hamman at 3:11 of the first round via knockout.\n\nThe final bout of the preliminary card consisted of light heavyweights [[Krzysztof Soszynski]] and [[Igor Pokrajac]]. Pokrajac immediately came after Soszynski with punches that staggered and backed Soszynski up to the cage. Soszynski fell to the ground under the rapid series of strikes and covered up to protect himself. Thirty-five seconds into the first round, referee Yves Lavigne stopped the fight, giving Igor Pokrajac the knockout victory.\n\n===Main card===\nThe first bout of the evening that aired on the pay-per-view broadcast was a featherweight bout between [[Mark Hominick]] and [[Chan Sung Jung]]. At the start, Hominick threw a wild left punch that missed Jung. Jung responded with a straight right that connected and sent Hominick to the ground. Jung followed Hominick down with follow-up punches that resulted in the referee stopping the match. In a tie for the second fastest official knockout in UFC history, Chan Sung Jung defeated Mark Hominick by knockout seven seconds into the first round.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26493/ufc-140-results-mark-hominick-vs-chan-sung-jung.mma |title=UFC 140 results: Chan Sung Jung stuns Mark Hominick with seven-second TKO |first1=Dann |last1=Stupp |first2=John |last2=Morgan |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=December 10, 2011 |access-date=April 1, 2012 |archive-date=March 18, 2012 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120318162857/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26493/ufc-140-results-mark-hominick-vs-chan-sung-jung.mma |url-status=dead }} The win also earned Jung the event's \"Knockout of the Night\" award.\n\nWelterweights [[Claude Patrick]] and [[Brian Ebersole]] then faced each other. The first two rounds of the fight consisted of a lot of [[clinch fighting]] up against the cage with occasional grappling on the ground. The third round occurred mostly on the ground, with Patrick making submission attempts at the start and end of the round, while in between Ebersole was on top striking down at his opponent. After all three rounds of the fight were completed, a split decision victory was given to Brian Ebersole.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2011/12/10/ufc-140-brian-ebersole-beats-claude-patrick-by-split-decision#2909996 |title=UFC 140: Brian Ebersole Beats Claude Patrick by Split Decision |first=Michael David |last=Smith |date=10 December 2011 |publisher=MMAFighting.com |access-date=April 1, 2012}}\n\n[[Tito Ortiz]] and [[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]] then participated in their light heavyweight fight. Ortiz early striking pushed Nogueira back. Nogueira managed to counter Ortiz's attack and reversed the situation, pushing Ortiz back. Nogueira then dropped Ortiz with a knee to the body and began pounding on him. Nogueira threw punches to Ortiz's body and when Ortiz failed to respond to the attack the referee stopped the fight. The result was Antônio Rogério Nogueira earning a technical knockout victory at 3:15 of the first round.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmamania.com/2011/12/11/2627641/ufc-140-results-recap-antonio-rogerio-nogueira-vs-tito-ortiz-fight |title=UFC 140 results recap: Antonio Rogerio Nogueira vs Tito Ortiz fight review and analysis |first=Brian |last=Hemminger |publisher=MMAMania.com |date=December 11, 2011 |access-date=April 1, 2012}}\n\nThe co-main event then occurred with heavyweights [[Frank Mir]] fighting [[Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira]]. During the first round, Nogueira hit Mir with a punch that staggered Mir. Nogueira furthered his attack by attempting to take Mir's back and apply a choke submission. After some [[ground and pound]] Mir escaped from under Nogueira, grabbed Nogueira's right arm and attempted a [[kimura lock]]. The pair rolled over several times as Nogueira attempted to escape the submission hold, but Mir held tightly onto Nogueira's arm. Nogueira refused to tap out to the submission attempt until Mir broke his arm. The official decision was that Frank Mir defeated Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira by technical submission due to the kimura at 3:38 of the first round. As a result of this fight, Mir was awarded the event's \"Submission of the Night\" and earned the distinction of not only being the first man to knock out Nogueira, but also the first to submit him.{{cite news |url=https://www.usatoday.com/sports/mma/post/2011-12-10/nogueira-mir-ufc-140/582590/1 |title=Mir breaks Nogueira's arm at UFC 140 |first=Sergio |last=Non |publisher=USA Today |date=December 10, 2011 |access-date=April 1, 2012}}\n\nThe last bout of the evening was the Light Heavyweight Championship bout, in which [[Lyoto Machida]] attempted to take the title away from [[Jon Jones]]. Throughout the first round and a half it was a fairly even fight. Jones threw kicks and other strikes at Machida and Machida effectively countered the attacks. In the second round, Jones opened a gash on Machida's forehead, at which point the referee, [[John McCarthy (mixed martial arts)|John McCarthy]], suspended the match for the cage-side doctor to look at it. The doctor cleared Machida to continue fighting. After the restart, Jones was able to stun Machida with a punch and was able to apply a standing [[guillotine choke]]. Machida refused to tap out and fell to the floor unconscious when Jones released him as the ref stopped the bout. Jon Jones retained his title via technical submission at 4:26 of the second round.{{cite news |url=http://www.chron.com/sports/article/Jones-retains-light-heavweyight-title-at-UFC-140-2395183.php |title=Jones retains light heavyweight title at UFC 140 |publisher=Houston Chronicle / AP |date=December 10, 2011 |access-date=April 1, 2012}} It became the first time that Machida had been submitted in a professional fight.{{cite news |url=http://sportsillustrated.cnn.com/2011/writers/jeff_wagenheim/12/11/ufc-140/index.html |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120113135759/http://sportsillustrated.cnn.com/2011/writers/jeff_wagenheim/12/11/ufc-140/index.html |url-status=dead |archive-date=January 13, 2012 |title=UFC 140: Jones slays 'The Dragon' to cap breakthrough year |first=Jeff |last=Wagenheim |date=December 11, 2011 |access-date=April 2, 2012}} This championship fight was also awarded the event's \"Fight of the Night\".\n\n==Aftermath==\n[[Rashad Evans]] was one of the opponents whom [[Jon Jones]] was initially reported to fight at this event. That match for the UFC Light Heavyweight Championship eventually occurred at ''[[UFC 145]]'' on April 21, 2012.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2012/1/28/2755574/ufc-145-jon-jones-vs-rashad-evans-main-event-set-for-atlanta-on-april |title=Jon Jones vs Rashad Evans main event set for Atlanta on April 21 |last=Myers |first=Thomas |publisher=MMAMania.com |date=January 28, 2012|access-date=January 28, 2012}}\n\n[[Frank Mir]]'s victory over [[Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira]] moved him one step closer to a championship fight of his own. April 4, 2012, [[Alistair Overeem]] was revealed to have failed his pre-fight drug test by the Nevada State Athletic Commission (NSAC). He tested positive for elevated levels of testosterone that exceeded a ratio of 10-to-1, which was over the allowed ratio of 6-to-1. On Friday April 20, 2012, UFC President Dana White confirmed that Overeem has been removed from his fight with Dos Santos and replaced by Frank Mir.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Web. 1 Dec. 2012. \n\n==Background==\nThis card marked the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]]'s debut on the [[Fox Broadcasting Company|Fox Network]]. [[Zuffa]] CEO [[Lorenzo Fertitta]] confirmed that the debut show would be a one-hour special with only the main event airing live on television, but a full preliminary card taking place beforehand.{{cite web |url=http://www.lasvegassun.com/news/2011/aug/18/ufc-on-fox-manny-pacquiao/ |title=Details emerge regarding first UFC on FOX event |publisher=lasvegassun.com|access-date=August 18, 2011}}{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25919/velasquez-vs-dos-santos-will-be-lone-fight-featured-on-ufc-on-fox-1-regardless-of-length.mma |title=Velasquez-Dos Santos will be lone fight on UFC on FOX 1, regardless of length |publisher=mmajunkie.com |access-date=November 2, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20111103201357/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25919/velasquez-vs-dos-santos-will-be-lone-fight-featured-on-ufc-on-fox-1-regardless-of-length.mma |archive-date=November 3, 2011 |df=mdy-all }}\n\n[[Fox Deportes]] aired the [[Clay Guida|Guida]] vs. [[Benson Henderson|Henderson]], [[Dustin Poirier|Poirier]] vs. [[Pablo Garza (fighter)|Garza]], [[Cub Swanson|Swanson]] vs. [[Ricardo Lamas|Lamas]], and [[DaMarques Johnson|Johnson]] vs. [[Clay Harvison|Harvison]] fights.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25948/in-addition-to-title-fight-fox-deportes-to-air-henderson-guida-and-poirier-garza.mma |title=In addition to title fight, FOX Deportes to air Henderson-Guida and 2011 |df=mdy-all }}\n \t\nThe winner of the Clay Guida/Benson Henderson fight determined who was next to get a title shot against [[Frankie Edgar]] (the reigning champion at the time), at ''[[UFC 144]]'' in [[Japan]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26042/ufc-on-foxs-guida-vs-henderson-winner-expected-to-face-champ-edgar-in-japan.mma |title=UFC on FOX's Guida vs. Henderson winner |df=mdy-all }}\n\nThe UFC on Fox main event broadcast marked the most-watched live hour ever for [[Mixed martial arts|MMA]] in the [[United States]] and averaged 5.7 million viewers.{{cite news 140|UFC 140: Jones vs. Machida]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC 142|UFC 142: Aldo vs. Mendes]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 141: Lesnar vs. Overeem''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] [[pay-per-view]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on Friday, December 30, 2011, at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]].{{cite web |url=https://www.ufc.com/event/UFC141#/fight|title=UFC 141: Lesnar vs. Overeem|publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship|date=November 7, 2011}}\n\n==Background==\nThe UFC returned to a 10 p.m. ET start with this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/25636/ufc-returning-to-10-p-m-et-pay-per-view-slot-with-ufc-141.mma|title=UFC returning to 10 p.m. ET pay-per-view slot with UFC 141|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2011-10-12|access-date=2011-10-12|author=Staff|archive-date=2011-10-13|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20111013022314/http://mmajunkie.com/news/25636/ufc-returning-to-10-p-m-et-pay-per-view-slot-with-ufc-141.mma|url-status=dead}}\n\n''UFC 141'' was the last event to feature preliminary fights live on [[Spike TV]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26298/ufc-141-ufc-prelims-special-gets-njokuani-nijem-assuncao-pearson-on-spike-tv.mma |title=UFC 141 'UFC Prelims' special gets Njokuani-Nijem, Assuncao-Pearson on Spike TV |publisher=[[MMAjunkie.com]] |date=November 29, 2011 |last=Stupp |first=Dann |access-date=November 29, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20130128233851/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2011/11/ufc-141-ufc-prelims-special-gets-njokuani-nijem-assuncao-pearson-on-spike-tv |archive-date=January 28, 2013 }}\n\nThis event marked [[Brock Lesnar]]'s return to MMA action after being out for over a year due to [[diverticulitis]]. The lead up to the fight was rife with controversy regarding his opponent, [[Alistair Overeem]]. In November 2011, both competitors were required by the [[Nevada Athletic Commission|NSAC]] to comply with out-of-competition drug testing. Lesnar delivered his sample screen on November 21, while Overeem delivered his on November 23. The screen, however, did not meet the standards of the commission. Overeem submitted a second test through his personal physician – which was also deemed unacceptable – before flying out of the country. Overeem was ultimately given a conditional license for the fight by the committee during a meeting held on December 12, 2011.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2011/12/alistair-overeem-granted-conditional-license-for-ufc-141-facing-four-drug-tests|title=Alistair Overeem granted conditional license for UFC 141, facing four drug tests|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2011-12-12|access-date=2011-12-12|author=Staff|archive-date=2023-11-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20231108233828/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2011/12/alistair-overeem-granted-conditional-license-for-ufc-141-facing-four-drug-tests|url-status=dead}}\n\nThe winner of the main event was expected to face [[Junior dos Santos]] in a bout for the [[List of UFC Champions#Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Championship]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/6937472/brock-lesnar-alistair-overeem-fight-set-dec-30-vegas|title=Brock Lesnar-Alistair Overeem fight set|work=ESPN.com|date=September 6, 2011|access-date=September 6, 2011|last=Okamoto|first=Brett}} After being defeated by Overeem, Lesnar announced his immediate retirement from the UFC.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/7406718/brock-lesnar-retires-first-round-loss-alistair-overeem-ufc-141|title = Lesnar retires after first-round loss to Overeem|date = 31 December 2011}}{{cite web | url=https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NxBNXdqjwsQ | title=- YouTube | website=[[YouTube]] }}\n\n[[T. J. Grant]] was originally scheduled to face [[Jacob Volkmann]] at this event but was forced to pull out due to an undisclosed injury, and was replaced by returning veteran [[Efraín Escudero]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26406/tuf-8-champ-escudero-likely-to-return-to-ufc-meet-volkmann-at-ufc-141.mma|title='TUF 8' champ Escudero returning to UFC, meets Volkmann at UFC [[UFC on FX: Guillard vs. Miller]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 142: Aldo vs. Mendes''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on January 14, 2012, at the [[HSBC Arena (Rio de Janeiro)|HSBC Arena]] in [[Rio de Janeiro]], [[Brazil]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/event/UFC142#/fight|title=UFC RIO: Aldo vs. Mendes|publisher=UFC.com|date=November 29, 2011}} It was the first UFC event of the year, and was broadcast live on [[pay-per-view]] in [[North America]].\n\n==Background==\n''UFC 142'' featured preliminary fights live on [[Sportsnet]] and [[FX (TV channel)|FX]].\n\n[[Siyar Bahadurzada]] was scheduled to face [[Erick Silva]], but pulled out of the bout.{{cite web|url=http://mmafrenzy.com/26926/ufc-142-loses-thiago-nedkov-broughton-and-bahadurzada/|title=UFC 142 Loses Thiago, Nedkov, Broughton, and Bahadurzada|work=mmafrenzy.com|date=December 16, 2011 |access-date=December 16, 2011}} Veteran fighter [[Carlo Prater]] stepped in as a replacement for Bahadurzada.{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/erick-silva-gets-new-opponent-for-ufc-rio-142|title=Erick Silva Gets New Opponent for UFC Rio 142 |work=mmaweekly.com|date=December 16, 2011 |access-date=December 16, 2011}}\n\n[[Rob Broughton]] was scheduled to face Ednaldo Oliveira, but pulled out of the bout. Recently resigned heavyweight [[Gabriel Gonzaga]] replaced Broughton in the bout.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26578/gabriel-gonzaga-returns-at-ufc-142-meets-ednaldo-oliveira-on-prelims.mma|title=Gabriel Gonzaga returns at UFC 142, meets Ednaldo Oliveira on prelims|work=mmajunkie.com|date=December 16, 2011|access-date=December 16, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://archive.today/20130129171723/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2011/12/gabriel-gonzaga-returns-at-ufc-142-meets-ednaldo-oliveira-on-prelims|archive-date=January 29, 2013}}\n\n[[Paulo Thiago]] was scheduled to face [[Mike Pyle (fighter)|Mike Pyle]], but pulled out of the bout due to an injury. Recently resigned welterweight [[Ricardo Funch]] replaced Thiago in the bout.{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/ricardo-funch-in-to-face-mike-pyle-at-ufc-142|title=Ricardo Funch In To Face Mike Pyle at UFC 142|work=mmaweekly.com|date=December 17, 2011 |access-date=December 17, 2011}}\n\n[[Stanislav Nedkov]] was scheduled to take on [[Fabio Maldonado]] at this event, but visa problems forced Nedkov to withdraw from the bout. Newcomer [[Caio Magalhaes]] stepped in as a replacement for Nedkov in late December.{{cite web|url=http://www.heavy.com/mma/mma-news/2011/12/newcomer-caio-magalhaes-meets-fabio-maldonado-at-ufc-142-in-rio/|title=Fabio Maldonado Draws Newcomer for UFC 142 |work=heavy.com|date=December 20, 2011 |access-date=December 20, 2011}} However, Maldonado was injured less than a week before his fight with Magalhaes and the bout was scratched from the card.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26878/fabio-maldonado-injured-in-training-ufc-142-fight-with-caio-magalhaes-scratched.mma|title=Fabio Maldonado injured in training, UFC 142 fight with Caio Magalhaes scratched|work=mmajunkie.com|date=January 8, 2012|access-date=January 8, 2012|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://archive.today/20130128221238/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/01/fabio-maldonado-injured-in-training-ufc-142-fight-with-caio-magalhaes-scratched|archive-date=January 28, 2013}}\n\nAt the UFC 142 weigh ins, [[Anthony Johnson (fighter)|Anthony Johnson]] failed to make the middleweight limit. Johnson came in 11 pounds over the 186 lb weight allowance, was fined 20 percent of his earnings and the bout was contested at a catchweight of 197 lb.{{cite web | url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26934/ufc-142-live-and-official-weigh-in-results.mma | title=UFC 142 weigh-in results: 'Rumble' Johnson 12 pounds heavy, fined 20 percent of purse | publisher=MMAJunkie.com | date=January 13, 2012 | access-date=January 13, 2012 | url-status=dead | archive-url=https://archive.today/20120710230858/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26934/ufc-142-live-and-official-weigh-in-results.mma | archive-date=July 10, 2012 }} However, Belfort requested that Johnson not weigh more than 205 lb the day of the fight. The UFC mandated that if Johnson could not make the 205 lb limit, the bout would be cancelled. Johnson officially weighed in at 204.2 lb on fight day, and the bout took place as scheduled.{{cite web | url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26975/anthony-johnson-weighs-204-2-pounds-ufc-142-co-main-event-is-on.mma | title=Anthony Johnson weighs 204.2 pounds, UFC 142 co-main event is on | publisher=MMAJunkie.com | date=January 14, 2012 | access-date=January 14, 2012 | url-status=dead | archive-url=https://archive.today/20120712111919/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26975/anthony-johnson-weighs-204-2-pounds-ufc-142-co-main-event-is-on.mma | archive-date=July 12, 2012 }}\n\n==Results== 142|UFC 142: Aldo vs. Mendes]]\n|followingevent=[[2012 in UFC#UFC on Fox: Evans vs. Davis|UFC on Fox: Evans vs. Davis]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on FX: Guillard vs. Miller''''' (also known as '''''UFC on FX 1''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on January 20, 2012 at the [[Bridgestone Arena]] in [[Nashville, Tennessee]].{{cite web |url=https://www.ufc.com/event/UFCFX1#/fight|title=UFC on FX: Guillard vs. Miller|publisher=ufc.com |date=November 28, 2011 |access-date=November 28, 2011}}\n\n==Background==\n\nThe event was the second to be broadcast as part of a seven-year agreement between the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] and [[Fox Broadcasting Company|Fox]], and the inaugural event to air on [[FX (TV channel)|FX]].{{cite web |url=https://msn.foxsports.com/boxing/story/FOX-UFC-announce-multi-year-deal-081811|title=FOX, UFC announce multiyear deal|publisher=msn.foxsports.com |date=August 18, 2011 |access-date=August 18, 2011}}\n\nThe [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]]'s second announce team made their debut at this event, featuring former [[Bellator Fighting Championship|Bellator]] announcer [[Jon Anik]] on play-by-play and [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] [[Lightweight (MMA)|lightweight]] [[Kenny Florian]] on color commentary. Anik and Florian had previously worked together as hosts of the [[ESPN2]] series [[MMA Live]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/27000/kenny-florian-tapped-color-commentator-for-ufc-broadcasting-b-team.mma|title=Kenny Florian tapped color commentator for UFC broadcasting 'B team'|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=January 16, 2012|access-date=January 22, 2012|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120119212715/http://mmajunkie.com/news/27000/kenny-florian-tapped-color-commentator-for-ufc-broadcasting-b-team.mma|archive-date=January 19, 2012}}\n\nA [[Featherweight (MMA)|featherweight]] bout between [[Charles Oliveira]] and [[Robert Peralta]] was briefly linked to this event,{{cite web|url=http://www.thecagedoor.net/media/news/breaking-news-charles-oliveiras-next-opponent-named|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20111120063929/http://www.thecagedoor.net/media/news/breaking-news-charles-oliveiras-next-opponent-named|url-status=dead|archive-date=November 20, 2011|title=Breaking News: Charles Oliveira's next opponent named|publisher=The Cage Door|date=November 16, 2011}} but it was later confirmed that Oliveira would instead be facing [[Eric Wisely]] a week later at [[UFC on Fox: Evans vs. Davis]].\n\n[[Rafaello Oliveira]] was expected to fight [[Reza Madadi]] at the event. However, after a hand injury, Oliveira pulled out of the bout and was replaced by [[Fabrício Camões]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26677/replacement-fabricio-camoes-steps-in-to-fight-reza-madadi-at-ufc-on-fx-1.mma|title=Replacement Fabricio Camoes steps in to fight Reza Madadi at UFC on FX 1|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=December 27, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120211073405/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26677/replacement-fabricio-camoes-steps-in-to-fight-reza-madadi-at-ufc-on-fx-1.mma|archive-date=February 11, 2012}} Madadi would later withdraw from the bout due to injury as well and was replaced by promotional newcomer Tommy Hayden.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26991/tommy-hayden-targeted-for-ufc-on-fx-1-slot-against-fabricio-camoes.mma|title=Tommy Hayden targeted for UFC on FX 1 slot against Fabricio Camoes|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=January 16, 19, 2012}}\n\n[[Mike Brown (fighter)|Mike Brown]] was expected to face [[Vagner Rocha]], but was forced out of the bout with an injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26735/mike-brown-scratches-from-ufc-on-fx-1-ricardo-lamas-out-of-ufc-143.mma|title=Mike Brown scratches from UFC on FX 1, Ricardo Lamas out of UFC 143|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=December 29, 2011|access-date=September 28, 2014|archive-date=October 25, 2012|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121025133425/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26735/mike-brown-scratches-from-ufc-on-fx-1-ricardo-lamas-out-of-ufc-143.mma|url-status=dead}} Rocha will instead face [[Jonathan Brookins]] at [[UFC on Fuel TV 1]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26837/ufc-on-fuel-tv-1-adds-dillashaw-vs-watson-brookins-vs-rocha.mma|title=UFC on FUEL TV 1 adds Dillashaw vs. Watson, Brookins vs. Rocha|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=January 5, 2012|access-date=January 5, 2012|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120508131401/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26837/ufc-on-fuel-tv-1-adds-dillashaw-vs-watson-brookins-vs-rocha.mma|archive-date=May 8, 2012}}\n\n[[Ken Stone (fighter)|Ken Stone]] was expected to fight [[Mike Easton]] at this event, but was forced from the bout due to injury and replaced by promotional newcomer Jared Papazian.{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/kotc-champ-jared-papazian-tabbed-to-face-mike-easton-at-ufc-on-fx-1|title=KOTC Champ Jared Papazian Tabbed to Face Mike Easton at UFC on FX 1 |publisher=mmaweekly.com|date=January 5, 2012|access-date=January 5, 2012}}\n\n[[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] newcomer [[Ryan Jimmo]] was expected to face [[Karlos Vemola]] at this event, but the bout was cancelled after Jimmo fell injured a week prior to the event.{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/jimmo-vs-vemola-off-ufc-on-fx-1-card-camoes-awaits-new-opponent|title=Jimmo vs. Vemola Off UFC on FX 1 Card; Camoes Awaits New Opponent |publisher=mmaweekly.com|date=January 14, 2012}}\n\nThis event featured the UFC debut of future [[List of UFC champions#Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]].\n\nThe preliminary card aired on [[Fox Sports 2|Fuel TV]].{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/ufc-on-fuel-tv-kicks-off-with-24-hour-marathon|title=UFC UFC]]\n}}\n\nThe year '''2012''' was the 20th year in the history of the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC), a [[mixed martial arts]] promotion based in the United States. 2012 started with [[UFC 142|UFC 142: Aldo vs. Mendes]] and ended with [[UFC 155|UFC 155: dos Santos vs. Velasquez 2]]. The year saw the successful UFC Featherweight title defense by [[José Aldo]], the crowning of [[Carlos Condit]] as the Interim Welterweight Champion, a new Lightweight Champion in [[Benson Henderson]], a tournament to crown the first UFC Flyweight Champion as well as the finales of ''[[The Ultimate Fighter 15]]'' and ''[[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil]]''.\n\n== 2012 UFC.com awards ==\n\n{| class=\"wikitable\" width=\"100%\" style=\"text-align:Center\"\n! colspan=\"7\" style=\"border: 1px solid black; padding: 5px; background: #3B99FA; color:inherit;\"|2012 UFC.COM Awards\n|-\n!No\n!Best Fighter\n!The \nUpsets\n!The \nSubmissions\n!The Newcomers\n!The \nKnockouts\n!The \nFights\n|-\n\n|1\n|style=\"font-size:87%\"|[[Benson Henderson]]\n|style=\"font-size:87%\"|[[Tim Boetsch]]

Evans vs. Davis\n|image=UFC_on_Fox_Evans_vs._Davis_poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date=January 28, 2012\n|venue=[[United Center]]\n|city=[[Chicago, Illinois]]\n|attendance=16,963{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/27233/ufc-on-fox-2-officially-draws-16963-attendance-and-1-27-million-gate.mma|title=UFC on FOX 2 officially draws 16,963 on FX: Guillard vs. Miller]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 143|UFC 143: Diaz vs. Condit]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on Fox: Evans vs. Davis''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fox 2''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on January 28, 2012, at the [[United Center]] in [[Chicago, Illinois]].{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/event/UFCFox2#/fight|title=UFC on Fox 2: Evans vs. Davis|publisher=ufc.com|access-date=December 7, 2011}}\n\n===Background===\nThe event was the first to be broadcast on [[Fox Broadcasting Company|Fox]] as part of a seven-year agreement between the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] and the network and the second to air live on [[Fox Broadcasting Company|Fox]] after the inaugural ''[[UFC on Fox: Velasquez vs. Dos Santos]]''.{{cite web |url=http://msn.foxsports.com/boxing/story/FOX-UFC-announce-multi-year-deal-081811|title=FOX, UFC announce multiyear deal|publisher=msn.foxsports.com |date=August 18, 2011 |access-date=August 18, 2011}}\n\nA bout between [[Demetrious Johnson (fighter)|Demetrious Johnson]] and [[Eddie Wineland]] was originally slated for this event. However, Johnson was pulled from the bout to be a participant in the UFC's [[Flyweight (MMA)|Flyweight]] tournament set to begin in March. Wineland was then expected to face [[Johnny Bedford]] at this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26536/tuf-14-fighter-johnny-bedford-takes-ufc-on-fox-2-slot-against-eddie-wineland.mma |title='TUF 14' fighter Johnny Bedford takes UFC on FOX 2 slot against Eddie Wineland |publisher=mmajunkie.com |date=December 14, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120108000248/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26536/tuf-14-fighter-johnny-bedford-takes-ufc-on-fox-2-slot-against-eddie-wineland.mma |archive-date=January 8, 2012 }} However, Wineland himself was forced out of the bout with an injury and replaced by promotional newcomer [[Mitch Gagnon]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/26696/replacement-mitch-gagnon-meets-johnny-bedford-at-ufc-on-fox-2.mma |title=Replacement Mitch Gagnon meets Johnny Bedford at UFC on FOX 2 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |date=December 28, 2011 |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120519102858/http://mmajunkie.com/news/26696/replacement-mitch-gagnon-meets-johnny-bedford-at-ufc-on-fox-2.mma |archive-date=May 19, 2012 }} Then just days before the event, the bout was cancelled due to an alleged visa issue for Gagnon.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/27122/bedford-gagnon-scratched-at-ufc-on-fox-2-beltran-johnson-promoted-to-fuel-tv.mma |title=Bedford-Gagnon scratched at UFC on FOX 2 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |date=January 25, 2012 |access-date=2012-12-15 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120510040102/http://mmajunkie.com/news/27122/bedford-gagnon-scratched-at-ufc-on-fox-2-beltran-johnson-promoted-to-fuel-tv.mma |archive-date=2012-05-10 |url-status=dead }}\n\n[[Paul Sass]] was expected to face [[Evan Dunham]], but was forced from the bout with an injury and replaced by [[Nik Lentz]].{{cite [[Chris Weidman]] would step in on 11 days notice to face Maia as Bisping's replacement.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/27017/chris-weidman-steps-in-to-face-demian-maia-at-ufc-on-fox-2.mma |title=Chris Weidman steps in to face Demian Maia at UFC on FOX 2 |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=January 17, 2012 |access-date=2012-12-15
